834 entries, atomic-commits to audience-audit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **atomic-commits**: Guides splitting work into atomic commits — one single type of change per commit, judged by the operation not the goal. Use whenever committing, staging, or organizing changes into commits, or when a diff mixes several kinds of change (behavior, refactor, rename, reformatting, extraction). Covers what makes a commit atomic, over- vs under-splitting, keeping a behavioral change together with its tests, which cleanups ride along vs split off, normalizing before extracting, ordering a series so each commit stands on its own, and why size is irrelevant. ([elnur/agent-skills](https://github.com/elnur/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/atomic-commits))
